# Open Items Ledger

> Single source of truth for every open/incomplete item in this project: bugs under
> investigation, partially-built features, unpushed branches/commits, and "documented
> gaps" other agents leave behind (in code comments, plan docs, or their own final
> reports). Maintained by the `backlog-tracker` sub-agent — see
> `.claude/agents/backlog-tracker.md`.
>
> **Every agent's Completion Report must append a row here for any unresolved gap,
> known limitation, or deferred decision it leaves behind** — not just mention it in
> the final report. See the "Completion Report" section in this project's `CLAUDE.md`.
>
> This file records **claims**, not proof. A row says "this was true as of
> last-checked" — it is not evidence that the item is still open today. Re-verify
> before acting on a row older than 24h.

First pass replaced the adjacency check with a lookahead accepting `,`/`}`/`]`/`:`/`"` as valid closers, which fixed that pattern but the user's own re-test of the live app surfaced a second, more common real-world case the first pass missed: a quoted word directly followed by a genuine Hebrew sentence comma (`סביב "קריסה", המדדים...` — from the record's actual unredacted `shortSummary`), where a bare `,` lookahead wrongly treated the comma as a real JSON separator and truncated the string. Second pass narrowed the `,` branch to require that a real JSON token (`"`, digit, `-`, `{`, `[`, `}`, `]`, `true`/`false`/`null`) actually follows the comma. Reproduced both failure shapes with synthetic fixtures (`unescapedQuotedPhrase`, `unescapedQuotedWordFollowedByComma` — the latter copied verbatim from the user's own unredacted repair-report excerpt) plus regression fixtures guarding the two pre-existing paths the fix must not break (`missingCommaBetweenStringValues`, `arrayOfHebrewStringsWithTrailingComma`). Since repeated charging wasn't reproducible, proceeded straight to the confirmation gate as instructed. **Fixed additively**, `src/components/dashboard/VideoDetailPanel.jsx` only (5 blocks tagged `COST-GUARD`, ~65 of the file's dirty lines — the rest is another concurrent session's unrelated uncommitted work, not touched): new `freshImportConfirmPending` state; the auto-run effect now only opens a Hebrew confirm dialog ("⚠️ ניתוח AI חדש — פעולה בתשלום... זו בקשה אמיתית בתשלום") instead of calling the pipeline directly; `handleConfirmFreshImportGate` runs the existing, unmodified pipeline; `handleCancelFreshImportGate` explicitly patches `pendingFreshImport:false, freshImportRequestedAt:null, freshImportSource:null` onto the current record (nothing else touched) and leaves it there — no analysis logic, prompts, chunking, or stored-data shape changed. **A real bug was found and fixed while building the cancel handler**: the first version used the pipeline's own `consumeFreshImportFlag` (a `delete`-based strip) and live-tested that "ביטול" left `pendingFreshImport:true` still in storage — root cause: `forceUpsertVideo` (`src/services/videoStorage.js:601`) persists via `{...storedRecord, ...record}`, and a `delete`d (missing) key is invisible to that spread, so it silently keeps the old `true`; only an explicit `false` override actually clears it through that function. Fixed and re-verified live (cancel now correctly persists `false`). | TRADINGBRAIN-GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-SCHEMA | Deferred code-level item, surfaced by a real-transcript gap audit (2026-09-10) against a live GEM output (not the shipped example) and applied to the GEM docs directly (no application source touched): `videoTabsConfig.js`'s `pickArray`/`pickStringAsArray` helpers (`:403-409`, `:421-427`) are "first-non-empty-wins, no merge" — this silently drops real content in 2 confirmed spots on the fundamental-GEM flat-JSON route: (1) `case 'summary'` (`:689`) reads `shortSummary` before `fullSummary`/`mainLesson`, so the latter two never render in tab 1 while `shortSummary` is populated (which the GEM instructions require); (2) `case 'mistakes'` (`:781-786`, critical line `:783`) reads `mistakesToAvoid` before `warnings`, so `warnings` never renders in tab 7 while `mistakesToAvoid` is populated (also always required). Both are real, `normalizeAiAnalysisResult()` genuinely writes all of `fullSummary`/`mainLesson`/`warnings` onto `video` (verified `src/services/videoAnalytics.js` field-mapping table, unchanged) — the loss happens one layer later, in tab-item extraction, not in parsing. **Doc-only workaround already applied** (this task): `docs/gems/GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-INSTRUCTIONS.md`/`GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-SCHEMA.md`/`GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-EXAMPLE.json` updated to stop asking the GEM for a separate `warnings` field (merge into `mistakesToAvoid` instead) and to require the central conclusion inside `shortSummary` itself; re-ran `node --import ./scripts/tmp/register-gem-verify-loader.mjs scripts/tmp/gem-fundamental-schema-verify.mjs` against the corrected example — clean pass, `mistakes` now shows 3/3 items (was 2/3 before the merge), zero dropped root keys. **Separately, also newly observed** (not previously documented): tab-7 items for `frameworks`/`checklists`/`mistakesToAvoid` are plain strings, so the fixed item template (concept/definition/validity/measurement/source) never renders as separated UI, and the embedded "מקור: HH:MM:SS" timestamp text is inert — `StaticVideoTimestampLink`'s `resolveStaticVideoTimestamp()` (`src/lib/staticVideoTimestamp.js:26-27`) requires the item to be an object with a numeric `startSeconds`/`timestampSeconds`/`exactStartSeconds` field and returns `null` immediately for a plain string; chapters (tab 2) already get a working timestamp link because `normalizeAnalysisChapters` turns them into objects with real `startSeconds`. **Two real code-level questions, neither decided, no code written for either**: (a) should `pickArray`/`pickStringAsArray` gain a merge-all variant for call sites where multiple source fields are meant to combine rather than fall back (`summary`/`mistakes` cases specifically — but not all futur call sites necessarily want that, needs a case-by-case read, not a blanket change); (b) should tab-7 items become structured objects (`{concept, definition, validWhen, invalidWhen, howToMeasure, startSeconds, sourceQuote}`) instead of template-formatted strings, which would both let the UI render the template's parts separately and activate `StaticVideoTimestampLink` for free. Owner's decision needed on scope/priority before either is attempted. | needs-user-decision | 2026-09-10 | gemini-integration-engineer |

| TRADINGBRAIN-GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-SCHEMA | **Stale-analysis root cause found, reproduced live, and fixed (2026-09-11)** — the reported video ("ניתוח ערך / פנדמנטלי חלק 2...") was not present in this session's browser profile (135 videos, no title match for any of "פנדמנטלי חלק 2"/"בינה המלאכותית"/"Micha"/"24.05" — confirmed via direct localStorage search, not a partial look), so the exact user record could not be inspected; the mechanism was instead isolated by direct code reading and confirmed with a live, controlled repro on a throwaway test video. **Root cause (confirmed, file:line)**: `resolveVideoChapters()` (`src/services/videoAnalytics.js:1883-1915`) only reads the freshly-pasted `video.chapters` when `isManualChapterSource(video.chapterSource)` is true (`:1889`, requires `chapterSource` to be exactly `'manual_transcript'`\|`'saved'`\|`'gems_analysis'`) — but the manual-paste code path (`normalizeAiAnalysisResult`, same file, the function whose return object feeds every GEMS paste outside the brief/macro-universalTabs branches) never set `chapterSource` at all, and a full-codebase grep confirmed `'gems_analysis'` was checked in 4 places and **written in zero**. So on any video that already carried non-empty `video.aiChapters` from an earlier automatic "AI ניתוח" run (or any other prior source), `resolveVideoChapters()` fell through to the `aiChapters` branch (`:1904-1907`) and ignored the fresh paste; `mergeChapterSources()` (`src/lib/chapterEnrichment.js:161-195`, "existing objects win every duplicate comparison") then combined the stale `aiChapters` with the fresh `gemChapters` extraction whenever their timings didn't collide within `CHAPTER_DUPLICATE_TOLERANCE_SECONDS`, producing an **interleaved mix of old and new chapters** rather than either a clean replace or a clean reject. **Live repro** (fresh test video, no prior automatic analysis): pasted GEM V1 (7 chapters, "Income Statement/Momentum"-style framework names) → applied correctly; pasted GEM V2 (5 chapters, "Foundation/Engine" names) over it with no intervening reload → storage and render both showed clean V2 (this simple case was NOT broken, structural investigation was required to find the actual trigger). Injected a synthetic `video.aiChapters` (7 old items, simulating a prior automatic-analysis run) via direct localStorage write, confirmed it survived a hard reload, then re-opened the same video: chapters tab showed `nativeChapters:7, gemChapters:5, finalChapters:8` with old and new chapter titles genuinely interleaved by time — the exact "old analysis showing alongside the pasted one" symptom the user described, reproduced on demand. **Fix** (`videoAnalytics.js`, `normalizeAiAnalysisResult`'s return object, ~12 lines added after the existing `chapters,` line): when the freshly-parsed `chapters` array is non-empty, also emit `aiChapters: chapters` (mirroring, not clearing, to sidestep `updateStoredVideo()`'s `hasNonEmptyChapters` guard at `src/services/videoStorage.js:271-280`, which silently strips an explicit `aiChapters: []` before it ever reaches the merge) and `chapterSource: 'gems_analysis'` — this makes a GEMS paste a genuine full replace for the chapters family specifically (both the shadow `aiChapters` field and the "is this a manual source" gate), without touching any other field or any brief/macro code path. **Live-confirmed fixed**: re-pasted the same V2 JSON with the fix active → `aiChapters` in storage now mirrors `chapters` exactly (5/5, titles match), `chapterSource: 'gems_analysis'`; chapters tab now shows `nativeChapters:5, gemChapters:5, finalChapters:5`, zero interleaving, zero stale titles — verified again after a hard reload (`page.goto`, not SPA nav). **Brief non-regression, structural**: the brief paste path (`_applyParsedGems`, `contentType === 'marketBrief'` branch, `VideoDetailPanel.jsx:6540-6593`) returns before ever reaching `normalizeAiAnalysisResult` — briefs cannot be affected by this fix, by construction, not by testing. **Verification**: `npm run build` exit 0; `chapter-timestamps-qa.mjs`, `static-video-ingestion-qa.mjs` both PASS; re-ran the prior round's own throwaway verify scripts (`scripts/tmp/gem-fundamental-kpi-density-verify.mjs`, `gem-fundamental-timed-item-verify.mjs`, via `node --import ./scripts/tmp/register-gem-verify-loader.mjs`) — both still pass unchanged, confirming this fix didn't disturb last round's `financialMetrics`/`valuation`/timed-item work. **Not diagnosed further, reported only per the task's own instruction**: the OTHER two symptoms the user reported on their real video (a stale `shortSummary`, and old dashboard names "Income Statement/Momentum" vs the pasted JSON's "Foundation/Engine") were NOT reproduced by a simple two-paste sequence on a fresh test video (a direct re-paste cleanly replaced `shortSummary`/`frameworks` every time, with no shadow-field equivalent to `aiChapters` for either field) — these two fields have no known multi-source-merge mechanism analogous to the chapters bug, so if the user's real video still shows stale values for them after this fix, the cause is most likely a **different, not-yet-isolated** failure (the paste silently not applying at all, e.g. via the same class of "background write clobbers a fresh save" hazard flagged in the prior round's ledger row below, or simply an earlier historical paste being mistaken for a more recent one) — needs the user to reproduce on their own real video with the fix in place and report whether the mismatch persists. | ready-for-user-qa | 2026-09-11 | gemini-integration-engineer |

| TRADINGBRAIN-GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-SCHEMA | **Item template rendering (table layout) + brain-recommendation badge, implemented and live-verified (2026-09-11)** — both changes scoped entirely to `src/components/dashboard/LearningTabContent.jsx` (the shared item-row renderer used by tab 3/4's fundamental sections **and** by the brief's own `SummaryBriefingView.jsx` — see the brief-risk note below), display-time only, the stored GEM string itself is never rewritten (confirmed: `formatted` still derives from the raw `sourceItem`/`formatItem()` output, only the render branch changed). **Template table layout**: new `parseTemplateItem(text)` splits on the literal `' · '` separator and requires every resulting segment to match `label: value`; if all segments parse, the first segment's value renders as a bold title (`<p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">`) and the rest as small `<dt>`/`<dd>` label/value rows beneath it (rows whose value is `-` are dropped, matching the "מתי זה לא תקף: -" convention already used when a field doesn't apply); anything that doesn't cleanly parse (including the pre-existing plain-sentence items in other tabs) falls back unchanged to the original `renderLinkedMarketText(text)` span — this is the exact fallback the task required, not a new empty state. Generic by design (keys off "first segment becomes the title", not a hardcoded "מושג" check), so it also handles the differently-shaped `promptTemplates` template (`מטרה: ... · פרומפט: "..." · מקור: ...`) without a second code path. **Brain-recommendation badge**: new `extractBrainBadge(text)` matches a trailing `🧠 מומלץ לשמור למוח · סיבה: <reason>` marker via regex, strips it from the text used for display/copy/save/bulk-select (applied once, in `LearningTabContent`'s own `formatted` mapping, so it's consistent everywhere `text` is consumed downstream — not just the visible span), and surfaces it as a small pill badge (`🧠 מומלץ לשמור למוח`, `title={reason}` for the tooltip) rendered above the item content; items without the marker are provably unchanged (verified: `formatted[i].brainReason` is `null` and the badge conditional doesn't render). **Live-verified together** on a real pasted `frameworks` item containing both the fixed template and the brain marker, plus a plain non-template sibling item and a `checklists` item with a real "מתי זה לא תקף" value: bold title + 4 labeled rows rendered correctly for the template item (the "-" row correctly absent), the plain item rendered unchanged with no title/rows, the badge appeared exactly once with the correct tooltip text, and the raw "· סיבה:" marker text was confirmed absent from the rendered DOM anywhere (`hasRawBadgeText: false`) — not just visually not obviously present. **Brief risk assessed, not live-verified — environment-blocked, disclosed rather than assumed clean**: `LearningTabContent` is also used by the brief's `SummaryBriefingView.jsx` (7 call sites — 30-second summary, market status, watch-today, key insights, risks, action checklist, executive conclusion) for real morning/evening brief content, so this change is NOT structurally isolated from the brief flow the way the same-round chapters fix is. Reasoned through instead of live-tested: `parseTemplateItem` requires the literal `' · '` separator (a deliberate, distinctive choice unique to this WORK-ID's fundamental-GEM template) AND every split segment to independently look like `label: value` — grepped every brief-content string formatter reachable from `SummaryBriefingView` (`src/lib/summaryBriefingDisplay.js`) and found exactly one that joins with `' · '` (`formatCalendarWatch`, `:61-69`, joining `[event, date, impact]`), but its first segment (`event`) is a free-text title with no colon, so it fails the `label: value` match immediately and `parseTemplateItem` returns `null` (safe fallback) for any realistic calendar-watch value — no other brief formatter (`formatIndexBullet`, `formatStockWatch`, plain prose insights/risks/checklist items) uses `' · '` at all. `extractBrainBadge` only fires on the exact literal Hebrew phrase "🧠 מומלץ לשמור למוח · סיבה:", a marker invented in this same session (item 3 of this round) exclusively for the fundamental/technical GEM instructions — it does not appear in any existing brief schema, prompt, or example. **Could not get a live brief render to confirm this directly**: the same pre-existing, unrelated `[MarketBriefPersistence] canonical write {"ok":false,"code":"indexeddb-active-generation-missing"}` blocker from the prior round's ledger row reproduced again on a fresh attempt (`indexedDB.databases()` → only `yt_mentor_app_data_v1` exists, its `meta` object store still reads back empty — confirmed again this round, not assumed carried over) — this is the app's real configured storage mode (`.env.development.local`: `VITE_YTMDB_STORAGE_MODE=indexedDB`), not a mode this session should route around by disabling IndexedDB, and the generation-initialization system itself is exactly the kind of migration-governance code a prior lesson (2026-08-28, `.codex/lessons.md`) warns against touching without a full-file read first — out of scope for this round, not attempted. No existing `market_brief_*` localStorage sidecar or real brief video with GEM-applied content was found anywhere in this browser profile to inspect read-only either. **Recommended follow-up**: the user should open one real, previously-analyzed morning or evening brief after this round's build and manually confirm tab 1 (30-second summary, market status, watch-today, insights, risks, checklist, executive conclusion) renders identically to before — specifically checking that no item unexpectedly renders as a bold-title/table instead of its normal sentence. **Verification**: `npm run build` exit 0; `dedicated-content-selection-qa.mjs` (13 structured types + generic renderer) PASS; `row-timestamp-annotation-qa.mjs` (both canonical and embedded-parity variants) PASS; `static-video-timestamp-qa.mjs` PASS. | needs-user-decision | 2026-09-11 | gemini-integration-engineer |

Confirmed this is a sound, not just convenient, gate: `extractUniversalTabContent()`'s first parameter is literally named `_video` and unused (`src/lib/universalTabSections.js:190`) — `mode:'sections'` is derived exclusively from `getUtRaw(marketBriefData, tabValue)` (`:137-145`, returns `undefined` immediately if `!marketBriefData?.universalTabs`), and `marketBriefData` is never populated by the flat/legacy paste branch fundamental/technical GEMs use (established earlier this WORK-ID) — so `hasStructuredSections` can only be true for brief/macro-sourced content, never for a fundamental/technical video, making this gate structurally exhaustive rather than a heuristic. **Live-verified** (real "הדבק JSON מ-GEMS"-pasted fixture, not synthetic DOM): 3 real tables rendered with the exact 3 named column headers, correct cell content, ℹ️ tooltip carrying exactly the leftover fields (`"מתי זה תקף: ...\nמקור: ..."`), 🧠 badge text `"🧠 מומלץ לשמור למוח"` confirmed via `textContent` (not just screenshot, which mis-rendered the emoji glyph), checkbox toggled the bulk-selection counter ("נבחרו 1") then untoggled cleanly, and the deliberately-non-matching sibling item ("פריט רגיל בלי תבנית שלא אמור להתפרק לטבלה") rendered in the plain fallback list below the table, not forced into a row — `outerHTML` of that plain row and of a table row's action cell compared directly and found to expose the identical action set (copy button only, in both cases — same underlying `saved`/`onBrain` conditions in both paths, proving `ItemRowActions` extraction changed nothing). **Brief non-regression**: `SummaryBriefingView.jsx` has zero diff this round (confirmed via `git diff --stat`) and never references `templateTable`, so its 7 `LearningTabContent` call sites keep the untouched default-`false` path unconditionally, independent of the gating fix above. Live-opened one real morning brief and one real evening brief (post-fix, hard `page.goto` reload): both correctly classified in-header ("מבזק בוקר —"/"מבזק ערב —"), tab 4 rendered its pre-existing unchanged empty-state text, zero console errors. **Could not go further — same pre-existing environment blocker as the prior 2 rounds**: neither brief video (nor any other video in this profile) has populated `marketBriefData`, so a live side-by-side of *populated* brief tab-4 content before/after this round's table code could not be captured — `indexedDB.databases()` still shows only `yt_mentor_app_data_v1` with empty stores, the same `indexeddb-active-generation-missing` condition documented in this WORK-ID's prior 2 rounds, not touched or worked around. Relying on the structural proof above (`hasStructuredSections`/`getUtRaw` chain) in place of a populated live comparison — flagged, not assumed. **Also observed, not this round's to fix**: the test fixture's "✅ צ'קליסטים" and "📋 צ'קליסט השקעה" sections both show the identical "יחס חוב להון" item — pre-existing duplicate test data from an earlier round's fixture setup, not something this round introduced or touched; resembles this WORK-ID's own duplication-audit topic (first round above) but on synthetic data, not a real GEM double-emit — worth a real-data recheck, not acted on here. **Verification**: `npm run build` exit 0 (both before and after the gating fix); `dedicated-content-selection-qa.mjs`, `static-video-timestamp-qa.mjs`, `test:row-timestamp-annotation` (both canonical and embedded-parity variants) all PASS, re-run after the fix. Fixed by broadening the branch trigger to `slug==='fundamental-analysis'||'technical-analysis'||(!slug && looksLikeFundamentalOrTechnical(effectiveVideo))`, a new narrow helper checking only the same exclusive field groups the header's pre-existing cross-content-signal chip already uses (`frameworks`/`analysisFrameworks`/`financialMetrics`/`valuation`/`investmentChecklist` for fundamental, `indicators`/`setups`/`tradingSetups`/`patterns`/`tradingPatterns` for technical) — deliberately excludes generic fields like `keyInsights`/`checklists` that any GEM output can populate, so a genuinely unrelated general video is not misrouted. **Audit performed before removing anything** (code-read + live-confirmed on the fixture): all 10 fields the old `Default` branch rendered as per-field cards are — with zero exceptions — *already* rendered unconditionally elsewhere, independent of which tab-7 branch fires: `trading-brain`'s constituent fields (`mainLesson`/`brainHighlights`/`analysis.brainHighlights`/`tradingPrinciples`/`mentalModels`/`keyInsights`) are fully covered by tab 3's existing `lesson`/`principles`/`insights`/`thesis` sections (`VideoDetailPanel.jsx` insights tab, `principles` already merges `tradingPrinciples+mentalModels+brainHighlights+analysis.brainHighlights` — confirmed this merge already existed in the current code, so **no new tab-3 section was needed**); `checklists`/`mistakes`/`valuation`/`financial-metrics` use the exact same `extractVideoTabItems` tabKey tab 4 already calls unconditionally (**these 4 were already duplicated between tab 4 and tab 7 today** — same function, same tabKey, called twice); `indicators`/`setups`/`patterns`/`cause-effect`/`market-impact` had **no tab-3/4 home at all** (closes the "still open" gap flagged in the 2026-09-10 row above) — added as 5 new always-computed tab-4 sections (`📈 אינדיקטורים`, `🎯 סטאפים`, `📊 פטרנים`, `🔗 סיבה ותוצאה`, `🌎 השפעה על השוק`), same pattern as the existing fundamental sections (no `alwaysShow`, hidden when empty like most tab-4 sections). The old `Default` branch's `defaultSectionDefs` array (10 per-field cards) was deleted entirely, replaced with the original plain "אין תוכן ייעודי לסרטון זה" empty message — safe by construction, not just in practice, since every field it used to render is now proven to have an unconditional home outside tab 7. **Bug found and fixed during this round's own live QA** (not by the user): first live test crashed React with "Maximum update depth exceeded" inside `MarketRegimeSection` → `useMorningBriefSectionEdit` → `useBriefSectionManualEdit`'s `useEffect([marketBriefData, editing])` — root cause: `useMorningBriefSectionEdit` (`MorningBriefPanels.jsx`) was passing `marketBriefData || {}`, a **fresh object literal on every render** when `marketBriefData` is null, so the effect's dependency never stabilized (setDraft → render → new `{}` → effect fires → setDraft → ...). This is a **pre-existing latent bug**, not something this round introduced — it would already crash tab 7 for any real, not-yet-analyzed מבזק בוקר/ערב video too (marketBriefData is null before the first GEM paste); this round's new routing is simply the first path to reliably exercise `MorningBriefDashboard` with `marketBriefData=null`. | YMD-SAVETRANSCRIPT-MISSING-COST-GUARD | ממצא חדש, לא קשור ל-`YMD-CHAPTERS-YOUTUBE-SOURCE` (עלה אגב אימות ששם: שאין קריאת-AI בתשלום שיכולה לירות אוטומטית). ב-`src/components/dashboard/VideoDetailPanel.jsx`: `handleSaveManualTranscript` (`:7443`) מחוברת ל-`onClick` של כפתור בדיאלוג "הדבק תמלול ידני" (`:12985`), עם תווית "שמור תמלול והרץ ניתוח AI" (`:12991`, אייקון Sparkles; במצב טעינה "שומר ומנתח..." `:12990`) — קוראת ללא-תנאי ל-`await runAiAnalysis({ force: true, manualTranscriptOverride: text })` (`:7461`), אותו מנגנון ניתוח Claude בתשלום שכפתור "נתח עם Claude" הנפרד משתמש בו. **תיקון לממצא המקורי, אומת ישירות בקוד**: הכפתור **כן** מתויג כמריץ ניתוח AI (לא "שמור תמלול" סתמי) — אך **אין** תיוג-עלות (לא מוזכר תשלום/מחיר) ואין שלב אישור נפרד: הלחיצה חסומה רק ע"י אורך-טקסט מינימלי (40 תווים) ומצב-שמירה-פעיל (`:12986`), ויורה מיידית את הקריאה בתשלום ללא דיאלוג-אישור שני. **בניגוד לתקדים הקיים באותו קובץ בדיוק**: `TRADINGBRAIN-FRESHIMPORT-COST-GUARD` (ר' שורה למעלה, `VideoDetailPanel.jsx` ~8253-8306) הוסיף דיאלוג-אישור מפורש ("⚠️ ניתוח AI חדש — פעולה בתשלום...") למניעת בדיוק תרחיש כזה — `handleSaveManualTranscript` נטול guard מקביל. **מאומת: קוד קיים-מראש, לא נגוע ע"י `YMD-CHAPTERS-YOUTUBE-SOURCE`** — `git diff` על 3 הקבצים שאותו WORK-ID נגע בהם (`src/services/youtubeMetadata.js`, `src/services/youtubeOEmbed.js`, `scripts/chapter-timestamps-qa.mjs`) מכיל אפס אזכורים של `handleSaveManualTranscript`/`runAiAnalysis`. **לא תוקן בסבב זה** — דורש החלטת מוצר לפני כל שינוי קוד: דיאלוג-אישור בסגנון ה-guard הקיים? הפרדת "שמירה" מ"ניתוח" לשתי פעולות נפרדות? תיוג-עלות על הכפתור הקיים? — כל האפשרויות פתוחות, טרם הוכרעו. | open | 2026-09-12 | unassigned |

| YMD-PAID-AI-CONFIRM-GATE | **ממצא חדש, לא כפול, נגזר מ-`YMD-SAVETRANSCRIPT-MISSING-COST-GUARD`** (אותה משימה שהעלתה את הממצא הבודד ב-`handleSaveManualTranscript` עשתה הפעם סקירת-מלאי מלאה של **כל עץ ה-src/** לכל נתיב קוד שמגיע לקריאת מודל בתשלום, כדי לתכנן gate-אישור אחד משותף וניתן לשימוש חוזר — לא לתקן. **מלאי נקודות-קריאה בתשלום (כל אחת אומתה מול קוד עדכני, שורות נבדקו טריות ע"י `backlog-tracker`)**: (1) `handleAnalyze`/`runClaudeAnalysisFromCard` (`VideoDetailPanel.jsx:8223`/`:3336`) → `runAiAnalysis` (`:7666`) → `analyzeVideoWithProvider` (`src/services/aiVideoAnalyzer.js:32`) → `analyzeVideoWithClaude` (`src/services/claudeVideoAnalyzer.js:47`) → `POST /api/claude-video-analyze`; כפתור "נתח עם Claude" (`:12170/12175`) — קליק מפורש בלבד, ללא דיאלוג-אישור, ללא תיוג-עלות, לא יורה אוטומטית. (2) אותו pipeline דרך `handleSaveManualTranscript` (`:7443/7461`, onClick `:12985`) — **כבר מכוסה ומתועד ב-`YMD-SAVETRANSCRIPT-MISSING-COST-GUARD`, לא משוכפל כאן, רק מקושר**. (3) אותו pipeline דרך `runFreshImportPipeline` (`:8109`) בשתי נקודות-כניסה שונות **עם רמות-הגנה שונות**: (א) `handleConfirmFreshImportGate` (onClick `:13592`) — מוגן היטב ב-`<Dialog>` קיים (`:13570-13599`, כותרת "⚠️ ניתוח AI חדש — פעולה בתשלום", גוף "ייבוא מחדש מאפס שולף תמלול טרי ומריץ ניתוח Claude חדש על הסרטון — זו בקשה אמיתית בתשלום ל-API, לא פעולה מקומית. להמשיך?", כפתור אישור "המשך לניתוח בתשלום", ביטול "ביטול", `onOpenChange` מטפל בסגירה-בלי-אישור כביטול — זהו התקדים הנכון הקיים, `TRADINGBRAIN-FRESHIMPORT-COST-GUARD`). (ב) **`handleFreshImportReset` (`:8623`, onClick `:10077`, תווית "🧹 מחק היסטוריה ונתח מחדש") קורא ל-`runFreshImportPipeline({resetBeforeAnalysis:true,...})` ישירות — עוקף לגמרי את דיאלוג-האישור, מאומת בקריאת קוד ישירה (`:8623-8637`) — קליק בודד יורה קריאת Claude אמיתית בתשלום, אפס אישור, אפס תיוג-עלות על הכפתור עצמו**. **פער אמיתי, לא תיאורטי בלבד**: השורה הקיימת `TRADINGBRAIN-GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-SCHEMA` (סבב 2026-09-11, מאומתת קיימת בטבלה זו) כבר מתעדת בדיוק את התרחיש הזה בפועל — לחיצה בודדת על אותו כפתור הפעילה ניתוח-AI אמיתי ולא-מדומה שהושלם בהצלחה, קריאת API אמיתית אחת שלא הייתה כוונת הלחיצה. (4) `handleGeminiContent` (`:8639`, 2 אתרי onClick — `:10285` ו-`:10918`) → `fetchGeminiVideoContent` → `POST /api/gemini-video-content`; תוויות "התחל ניתוח" ו-"🚀 הרץ ניתוח מבזק" — אף אחת אפילו לא אומרת "AI", לא כל שכן עלות; ללא דיאלוג-אישור; קליק מפורש בלבד. (5) `handleGenerateHebrewTitles` (`:4181`, onClick `:11288`) → `fetchHebrewChapterTitles` → `POST /api/gemini-hebrew-titles`; תווית "🤖 צור כותרות AI בעברית" — מתויג כ-AI, ללא תיוג-עלות, ללא דיאלוג-אישור. (6) `handleAiRepairGemsJson` (`:6856`, onClick `:13988`) → `requestGemsJsonRepair` → `POST /api/gemini-repair-json`; תווית "🤖 תקן JSON עם AI + הפק דוח" — כבר מבצע תיקון מקומי/דטרמיניסטי קודם ופונה ל-AI רק כ-fallback (נוהג טוב קיים) — עדיין ללא תיוג-עלות/דיאלוג-אישור. (8) `handleAnalyzeVideo` (`src/pages/TopicLearningPage.jsx:612`, 4 אתרי onClick: `:1267/1521/1601/1941`) → `analyzeVideoWithAI` (`src/api/functions.js`) → `POST /api/analyze-video`; **עמוד נפרד לגמרי מ-`VideoDetailPanel.jsx`** — אותה חשיפה בסיסית של ניתוח בתשלום, ללא דיאלוג-אישור, ללא תיוג-עלות; גם לא חסום ע"י אילוץ הקובץ המוגן. (9) `generateRowTimestamps` (`src/services/rowTimestamps.js:151`) → `POST /api/generate-row-timestamps`, נקרא מ-`src/components/dashboard/RowTimestampGenerator.jsx` (גם לא `VideoDetailPanel.jsx`) — **זהו כבר יישום-הייחוס הטוב ביותר בכל בסיס-הקוד**: `startConfirm` (onClick `:245`) פותח בלוק-אישור פנימי דו-שלבי עם טקסט מפורש (`:303`) "הפעולה תשלח בקשת AI אחת ל-Claude/Anthropic ועשויה להיות בתשלום ולצרוך מכסת Base44. שליפת התמלול עצמה אינה משתמשת ב-AI." בתוספת "התוכן הקיים לא יוחלף." (`:304`) — רק `runGeneration` (`:307`, הלחיצה השנייה בפועל) יורה את הקריאה בתשלום; `cancelConfirm` (`:310`) משאיר הכול ללא שינוי. זה צריך להיות המודל שממנו מכלילים את ה-gate המשותף. (10) **`legacyHandleAnalyze` (`VideoDetailPanel.jsx:7519`, קורא ל-`analyzeVideoWithAI`) ו-`fetchGeminiBasicSummary`/`generatePoliticalReplyFromBrain`** — **קוד מת מאומת** (grep מלא ב-src: אפס קריאות בפועל לאף אחת מהשלוש מלבד ההגדרה עצמה) — לא סיכון חי, לתיעוד בלבד. **הממצא החמור ביותר — סעיף נפרד, לא קבור ברשימה למעלה**: `handleGeneratePoliticalSummary` (`:4413`, פוגע ב-`POST /api/political-summary`) **נקרא אוטומטית מתוך `useEffect`** (`:4462-4511`, ההערה ב-`:4460` אכן כתובה "Auto-generate political summary when video opens and has transcript but no stored summary"), עם מערך-תלויות `[video?.id, video?.youtubeId, effectiveGemInfo?.gemKey, resolvedVideoMode.mode]` (`:4511`, מאומת מול קוד עדכני) — כלומר יורה בפתיחת/mount של הווידאו, **אפס קליק, אפס אישור, אפס תיוג-עלות**, בכל פעם שווידאו מסווג כ-political-mode (`resolvedVideoMode.mode==='politics' && effectiveGemInfo?.gemKey==='political'`), יש לו תמלול, ואין עדיין מפתח `political_summary_<id>` ב-localStorage. **זו הפרה חיה ופעילה עכשיו** של הכלל המפורש של הפרויקט (לעולם לא להריץ אוטומטית נתיב בתשלום ב-open/mount/import/refresh/retry/fallback) — שונה קטגורית מכל שורה אחרת למעלה (שהן קליק-מפורש-אך-לא-מתויג, לא auto-fire). דורש טריאז' עדיפות עצמאי, בלי קשר לבניית ה-gate המשותף. **מפרט ל-gate אישור משותף (לתיעוד בלבד, לא לבנייה)**: קומפוננטה/hook אחד לשימוש חוזר (למשל `usePaidAiConfirm()` + `<PaidAiConfirmDialog>`), מוכלל מ-2 הדפוסים הנכונים הקיימים למעלה (האישור-הפנימי-עם-טקסט-עלות-מפורש של `RowTimestampGenerator.jsx`, ומבנה `<Dialog>` כותרת/תיאור/אישור/ביטול של fresh-import עם `onOpenChange` בטוח) — פרמטרים: שם פעולה, מה תבצע, לאיזה וידאו/שורה זה חל, ו-callback `onConfirm`. כלל מחייב: כל שלב הרסני (מחיקה/reset) שפעולה הייתה מבצעת אחרת חייב לרוץ רק בתוך `onConfirm`, לעולם לא לפניו. `RowTimestampGenerator.jsx`/`AiMappingModal.jsx`/`TopicLearningPage.jsx` **אינם** `VideoDetailPanel.jsx` — הקומפוננטה המשותפת יכולה להיחלץ ואפילו לחוברת חלקית לתוך 3 הקבצים האלה בלי לגעת בקובץ המוגן כלל; רק אתרי-הקריאה ב-`VideoDetailPanel.jsx` (הרוב) דורשים אותו קובץ, ולכן השורה חסומה (`blocked`) בכללותה. **חסימה**: יישום מלא דורש עריכת `src/components/dashboard/VideoDetailPanel.jsx`, המוגן לפי `CLAUDE.md` של הפרויקט הזה וכרגע נושא עבודה בלתי-קשורה שאינה-committed מ-`TRADINGBRAIN-GEM-FUNDAMENTAL-SCHEMA` (מאומת טרי ע"י `backlog-tracker`: `git diff HEAD -- VideoDetailPanel.jsx` מציג diff קטן — 24 תוספות/15 מחיקות — כולו סביב prop `videoId` ל-`SummaryBriefingView` וסידור-מחדש של סעיפי טאב-4 [`alwaysShow`/`emptyLabel`], **אפס חפיפה** עם כל אחד מנתיבי-הקריאה בתשלום שנסקרו כאן). לא הותחל — סבב זה היה מלאי+מפרט בלבד, אין קוד שנכתב, אין קובץ שנגע מלבד ה-ledger הזה. | blocked | 2026-09-12 | gemini-integration-engineer |

| YMD-POLITICAL-TAB-VALUES-CRASH | **HIGH SEVERITY — live, reproducible production crash** (no severity column in this schema, flagged here instead). Opening ANY video whose `category` === `"פוליטיקה"` (`POLITICS_CATEGORY`, `VideoDetailPanel.jsx:1056`; `resolvedVideoMode.mode === "politics"`, set at `:2904-2913`) throws an uncaught `ReferenceError: POLITICAL_TAB_VALUES is not defined` inside a `useEffect` at `:3064-3081`, thrown specifically at `:3068`: `...(resolvedVideoMode.mode === "politics" ? ["political", ...POLITICAL_TAB_VALUES] : [])`. Fresh repo-wide `Grep` for the exact identifier confirms exactly one occurrence in the whole repo (this line) — `POLITICAL_TAB_VALUES` is never defined/declared/imported anywhere; no obvious drop-in replacement exists among nearby political-tab identifiers (`activePoliticalTab`/`showMorePoliticalTabs` :2263-2264, `hasPoliticalTabSet` :3038, `POLITICAL_PATHS` :4537). Independently confirmed pre-existing in committed HEAD (not this session's WIP): `git show HEAD:src/components/dashboard/VideoDetailPanel.jsx | grep -n POLITICAL_TAB_VALUES` against current HEAD `b4dbf2c` (branch `feat/saved-market-rows-table`, fresh `git rev-parse --short HEAD`) shows the identical broken line; the file's current uncommitted WIP diff (`git diff --stat HEAD`, 30 ins/19 del) is unrelated to this defect. Discovered live via Playwright (localhost:5184) while verifying an unrelated fix, `YMD-POLITICAL-AUTOFIRE-STOP`: opening an injected political-category test video threw this `ReferenceError` inside `commitHookEffectListMount`/`commitPassiveMountEffects` (full React stack trace naming `VideoDetailPanel`); reproduced on a 2nd, differently-shaped political test video (has `keyInsights`, no transcript); a non-political video in the same session produced 0 console errors across 189 captured messages, isolating the trigger to `mode === "politics"` specifically (reported by the discovering session; not independently re-run by `backlog-tracker`, which has no browser tool). **Unconfirmed hypothesis, flagged not proven**: because this crashing effect (`:3064`) is ordered ahead of other `useEffect`s later in this large component (e.g. the political-summary auto-effect, `:4466-4513`), the synchronous throw may be silently blocking those downstream effects for every political video, not just the AI-summary one — neither expected signal (`[PoliticalSummary]`-prefixed log, `political_summary_<id>` localStorage key) fired for either test video in live testing, including the case designed to hit the free/local-only fallback branch. Needs a code read of every `useEffect` after `:3068` in this file (or real DevTools) to confirm actual blast radius — not done here, out of scope for a record-only append. Kept as its own row rather than folded into `YMD-POLITICAL-AUTOFIRE-STOP`: distinct, pre-existing defect found incidentally while that fix's edit scope was deliberately limited to stopping the autofire only. | open | 2026-09-12 | unassigned |

| YMD-PAID-AI-CONFIRM-GATE | **סבב מעקב 2026-09-12 #2 — אימות צד-שרת + פריט קוד-מת חדש + תיקון עובדתי לפריט הפוליטי (רישום בלבד, לא לבנייה)**: (1) **אימות שרת, אין נתיב-קריאה בתשלום חי חדש**: `vite.config.js` רושם 20 מסלולי `/api/*` בסה"כ (grep טרי, כולם `server.middlewares.use`); 9 מהם נוגעים במודל — `analyze-video`, `gemini-video-content`, `gemini-repair-json`, `generate-row-timestamps`, `claude-video-analyze` (+`claude-video-analyze/status`, בדיקת-config בלבד, לא קריאה בתשלום), `political-summary`, `gemini-hebrew-titles`, `gemini-ai-mapping-diagnosis`; 11 האחרים (rss/market-fear-greed/resolve-channel/youtube-transcript/youtube-video-metadata/vault/*) אינם נוגעים במודל. מתוך 6 `backend/*.function.js` (`fetch-rss`,`resolve-channel`,`analyze-video`,`fetch-fear-greed`,`generate-row-timestamps`,`repair-gems-json`), grep טרי ל-`anthropic|gemini|ANTHROPIC_API_KEY|GEMINI_API_KEY|CLAUDE_MAX_TOKENS|CLAUDE_SYSTEM_PROMPT` אישר בדיוק 3 קוראים למודל: `analyze-video`, `repair-gems-json`, `generate-row-timestamps`. כל 9+3 כבר ממופים בסבב הקודם — אין נתיב חדש. ## Field reference

- **WORK-ID** — `YMD-<SLUG>` when the item already has one (from `docs/work-ledger.md`, a branch name, or a plan doc); otherwise `backlog-tracker` mints one in the same convention when it first records the item.
- **status** — exactly one of: `open` (known, not yet acted on) · `blocked` (cannot proceed without something external — a decision, a dependency, an environment fix) · `awaiting-push` (work exists — committed locally or on another branch/worktree — but is not merged/pushed to where it needs to be) · `needs-user-decision` (a concrete choice is on the table and only the user can make it).
- **last-checked** — `YYYY-MM-DD`, the date `backlog-tracker` (or the agent that filed the row) last confirmed the row's facts against real state (git, code, or the source doc). Not the date the item was first discovered.
- **owning agent** — the sub-agent whose domain the fix/decision belongs to, or `unassigned` if none fits or a human decision is required.
